If the AAT suffers a damage result of Heavy or higher from a rear attack, the power plant will explode within 2D turns. This explosion will destroy any droids and/or characters on board and completely gut the AAT. Secondly, the AAT's electrical systems are poorly shielded. If damage from ion weapons (or Gungan energy balls) exceeds the AAT's Body roll by more than 9 points, the electrical system shorts out.
